From Wikipedia, the free encyclopedia
This is a [dynamic list](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:WikiProject_Lists#Dynamic_lists "Wikipedia:WikiProject Lists") and may never be able to satisfy particular standards for completeness. You can help by [editing the page](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Special:EditPage/List_of_conspiracy_theories "Special:EditPage/List of conspiracy theories") to add missing items, with references to [reliable sources](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Wikipedia:Reliable_sources "Wikipedia:Reliable sources").
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/3/3d/Dollarnote_siegel_hq.jpg/250px-Dollarnote_siegel_hq.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Dollarnote_siegel_hq.jpg)The [Eye of Providence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eye_of_Providence "Eye of Providence"), or the all-seeing eye of God, seen here on the US$1 bill, has been taken by some to be evidence of a conspiracy involving the [Founding Fathers of the United States](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Founding_Fathers_of_the_United_States "Founding Fathers of the United States") and the [Illuminati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illuminati "Illuminati").[[1]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Barkun2003-1): 58 [[2]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-2): 47–49 
This is a list of notable [conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theory "Conspiracy theory"). Many conspiracy theories relate to supposed clandestine government plans and elaborate murder plots.[[3]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Harambam-Aupers_2021-3) They usually deny consensus opinion and cannot be proven using [historical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Historical_method "Historical method") or [scientific methods](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scientific_method "Scientific method"), and are not to be confused with research concerning verified [conspiracies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy "Conspiracy"), such as [Germany's pretense for invading Poland in World War II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Operation_Himmler "Operation Himmler"). 
In principle, conspiracy theories might not always be false, and [their validity depends on evidence](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falsifiability "Falsifiability") as for any theory. However, they are often implausible _[prima facie](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prima_facie "Prima facie")_ due to their [convoluted and all-encompassing nature](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Occam%27s_razor "Occam's razor").[[3]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Harambam-Aupers_2021-3) Conspiracy theories tend to be internally consistent and correlate with each other;[[4]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-4) they are generally designed to resist [falsification](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Falsifiability "Falsifiability") either by evidence against them or a lack of evidence for them.[[5]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-5)
Psychologists sometimes attribute proclivities toward conspiracy theories to a number of psychopathological conditions such as [paranoia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paranoia "Paranoia"), [schizotypy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Schizotypy "Schizotypy"), [narcissism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Narcissism "Narcissism"), and [insecure attachment](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Insecure_attachment "Insecure attachment"),[[6]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Andrade2020-6) or to a form of [cognitive bias](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cognitive_bias "Cognitive bias") called "[illusory pattern perception](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illusory_pattern_perception "Illusory pattern perception")".[[7]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-7)[[8]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-8) However, the current [scientific consensus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Scientific_consensus "Scientific consensus") holds that most conspiracy theorists are not pathological, but merely exaggerate certain cognitive tendencies that are universal in the human brain and probably have deep [evolutionary origins](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evolutionary_psychology "Evolutionary psychology"), such as natural inclinations towards [anxiety](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evolutionary_origin_of_anxiety "Evolutionary origin of anxiety") and [agent detection](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Agent_detection "Agent detection").[[6]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Andrade2020-6)
## Alternative history theories
Main article: [Pseudohistory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pseudohistory "Pseudohistory")
### False historical timelines
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/5b/Silvester_II_%28Saint_Paul_Outside_the_Walls%29.png/250px-Silvester_II_%28Saint_Paul_Outside_the_Walls%29.png)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Silvester_II_\(Saint_Paul_Outside_the_Walls\).png)[Pope Sylvester II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Sylvester_II "Pope Sylvester II") (from 999 to 1003)
Some theories claim that the dates of historical events have been deliberately distorted. These include the [phantom time hypothesis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Phantom_time_hypothesis "Phantom time hypothesis") of German conspiracy theorist[[9]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-9) Heribert Illig, who in 1991 published an allegation that 297 years had been added to the calendar by establishment figures such as [Pope Sylvester II](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Sylvester_II "Pope Sylvester II") in order to position themselves at the [millennium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Millennialism "Millennialism").[[10]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-10)
A comparable theory, [new chronology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_chronology_\(Fomenko\) "New chronology \(Fomenko\)"), is associated with Russian theorist [Anatoly Fomenko](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anatoly_Fomenko "Anatoly Fomenko"), who holds that history is many centuries shorter than widely believed, numerous historical documents have been fabricated, and legitimate documents destroyed, all for political ends. Adherents have included chess grandmaster [Garry Kasparov](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Garry_Kasparov "Garry Kasparov").[[11]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-11)
Main article: [Pre-Māori settlement of New Zealand theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pre-M%C4%81ori_settlement_of_New_Zealand_theories "Pre-Māori settlement of New Zealand theories")
[New Zealand](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Zealand "New Zealand") was first inhabited by indigenous [Māori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M%C4%81ori_people "Māori people") who arrived from [Polynesia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polynesia "Polynesia") in the 14th century AD.[[12]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-12)[[13]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-13)
The [Moriori](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Moriori "Moriori") were a group of Polynesians who left the New Zealand mainland for the [Chatham Islands](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chatham_Islands "Chatham Islands") in 1500 AD.[[14]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-14) Some historians in the 1800s proposed that the Moriori were a pre-Māori civilization which some theorists still believe to be true despite it being disproven.[[15]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-15)
[Māori mythology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/M%C4%81ori_mythology "Māori mythology") tells of races such as the [patupaiarehe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Patupaiarehe "Patupaiarehe") living in New Zealand before Māori.[[16]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-16) Other [conspiracy theorists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theory "Conspiracy theory") have suggested New Zealand was colonised by groups like the [Chinese](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_people "Chinese people") and [Spanish](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Spaniards "Spaniards").[[17]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-17)
Proponents of these theories may rely on [pseudo-archaeological](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pseudoarchaeology "Pseudoarchaeology") findings. Findings of [Kiore rat](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Polynesian_rat "Polynesian rat") fossils found beneath [tephra](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tephra "Tephra") of the [Taupō Volcanic eruption](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hatepe_eruption "Hatepe eruption")[[18]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-18) were unable to be replicated elsewhere.[[19]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-19) Others have interpreted natural geological rock formations such as the [Kaimanawa Wall](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kaimanawa_wall "Kaimanawa wall") as [pyramids](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pyramid "Pyramid").[[20]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-20)
### Tartaria
Main article: [Tartarian Empire](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tartarian_Empire "Tartarian Empire")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/0/05/1680_tartaria_map.jpg/250px-1680_tartaria_map.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:1680_tartaria_map.jpg)A 1680 map depicting an area labelled as Tartaria. Proponents of the theory suggest that the area shown is evidence of the Kingdom of [Tartaria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tartarian_Empire "Tartarian Empire"), whilst others refute this saying that it simply depicts [Tartary](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tartary "Tartary") which is an area not part of any kingdom.[[21]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-21)
Another claim is that world governments have hidden evidence for an advanced worldwide civilization with access to free energy and partially populated by giants called Tartaria, which was destroyed in the 1800s by a great "mud flood" cataclysm, causing its remains to be buried.[[22]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-22)
## Aviation
Numerous conspiracy theories pertain to air travel and aircraft. Incidents such as the 1955 bombing of the _[Kashmir Princess](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kashmir_Princess "Kashmir Princess")_ , the 1985 [Arrow Air Flight 1285](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Arrow_Air_Flight_1285 "Arrow Air Flight 1285") crash, the [1986 Mozambican Tupolev Tu-134 crash](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1986_Mozambican_Tupolev_Tu-134_crash "1986 Mozambican Tupolev Tu-134 crash"), the 1987 [_Helderberg_ disaster](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Helderberg_disaster "Helderberg disaster"), the 1988 bombing of [Pan Am Flight 103](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pan_Am_Flight_103_conspiracy_theories "Pan Am Flight 103 conspiracy theories") and the 1994 [Mull of Kintyre helicopter crash](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/1994_Scotland_RAF_Chinook_crash "1994 Scotland RAF Chinook crash") as well as various aircraft technologies and alleged sightings, have all spawned theories of foul play that deviate from official verdicts.[[23]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-23)
### Black helicopters
Main article: [Black helicopter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Black_helicopter "Black helicopter")
This conspiracy theory emerged in the US in the 1960s. The [John Birch Society](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Birch_Society "John Birch Society") originally promoted[[24]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Financial_Times-24) it, asserting that a [United Nations](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_Nations "United Nations") force would soon arrive in black helicopters to bring the US under UN control. A similar theory concerning so-called "phantom helicopters" appeared in the UK in the 1970s.[[25]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-25) The theory re-emerged in the 1990s during the presidency of [Bill Clinton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_Clinton "Bill Clinton"), and was "energetically" promoted by writer [Jim Keith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jim_Keith "Jim Keith") in his book _Black Helicopters Over America_. By the 2000s, the term "black helicopters" became a shorthand for anti-government conspiracy theories that "stretch the bounds of credulity",[[26]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Goldwag-26) such as those espoused by militia groups and a number of guests of talk show host [Glenn Beck](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glenn_Beck "Glenn Beck").[[27]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-27)[[28]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-28)
### Chemtrails
Main article: [Chemtrail conspiracy theory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chemtrail_conspiracy_theory "Chemtrail conspiracy theory")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/47/Contrail.fourengined.arp.jpg/250px-Contrail.fourengined.arp.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Contrail.fourengined.arp.jpg)A high-flying jet's engines leaving a condensation trail ([contrail](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contrail "Contrail"))
Also known as SLAP (Secret Large-scale Atmospheric Program), this theory alleges that water condensation trails ("[contrails](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contrails "Contrails")") from aircraft consist of chemical or biological agents, or contain a supposedly toxic mix of [aluminum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Aluminum "Aluminum"), [strontium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Strontium "Strontium") and [barium](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Barium "Barium"),[[29]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-29) under secret government policies. An estimated 17% of people globally believe the theory to be true or partly true. In 2016, the [Carnegie Institution for Science](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Carnegie_Institution_for_Science "Carnegie Institution for Science") published the first-ever peer-reviewed study of the chemtrail theory; 76 out of 77 participating atmospheric chemists and geochemists stated that they had seen no evidence to support the chemtrail theory or stated that chemtrail theorists rely on poor [sampling](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sampling_\(statistics\) "Sampling \(statistics\)").[[30]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-30)[[31]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-The_Daily_Telegraph-31)
### Korean Air Lines Flight 007
The destruction of [Korean Air Lines Flight 007](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korean_Air_Lines_Flight_007 "Korean Air Lines Flight 007") by Soviet jets in 1983 has long drawn [the interest of conspiracy theorists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Korean_Air_Lines_Flight_007_alternative_theories "Korean Air Lines Flight 007 alternative theories"). The theories range from allegations of a planned espionage mission, to a US government cover-up, to the consumption of the passengers' remains by giant crabs.[[32]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-32)
### Malaysia Airlines Flight MH370
See also: [Malaysia Airlines Flight 370 disappearance theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malaysia_Airlines_Flight_370_disappearance_theories "Malaysia Airlines Flight 370 disappearance theories")
The disappearance of [Malaysia Airlines Flight 370](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malaysia_Airlines_Flight_370 "Malaysia Airlines Flight 370") in southeast Asia in March 2014 has prompted many theories. One theory suggests that this plane was hidden away and reintroduced as [Flight MH17](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malaysia_Airlines_Flight_17 "Malaysia Airlines Flight 17") later the same year in order to be shot down over Ukraine for political purposes. American conspiracy theorist [James H. Fetzer](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/James_H._Fetzer "James H. Fetzer") has placed responsibility for the disappearance with the then Israeli prime minister [Benjamin Netanyahu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Benjamin_Netanyahu "Benjamin Netanyahu").[[33]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-The_Guardian-33) Historian [Norman Davies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Norman_Davies "Norman Davies") has promoted the conspiracy theory that hackers remotely took over a [Boeing Uninterruptible Autopilot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uninterruptible_autopilot "Uninterruptible autopilot"), supposedly installed on board, remotely piloting the aircraft to Antarctica.[[34]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-34)[[35]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-35)
### Malaysia Airlines Flight MH17
[Malaysia Airlines Flight 17](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malaysia_Airlines_Flight_17 "Malaysia Airlines Flight 17") was shot down over Ukraine in July 2014. This event has spawned numerous alternative theories. These variously include allegations that it was secretly [Flight MH370](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Flight_MH370 "Flight MH370"), that the plane was actually shot down by the [Ukrainian Air Force](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ukrainian_Air_Force "Ukrainian Air Force") to [frame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frameup "Frameup") Russia, that it was part of a conspiracy to conceal the "truth" about [HIV](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/HIV "HIV") (seven disease specialists were on board), or that the [Illuminati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Illuminati "Illuminati") or Israel was responsible.[[33]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-The_Guardian-33)[[36]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Sydney_Morning_Herald-36)
## Business and industry
### _Deepwater Horizon_
Multiple conspiracy theories pertain to [a fatal oil-rig industrial accident](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Deepwater_Horizon_explosion "Deepwater Horizon explosion") in 2010 in the [Gulf of Mexico](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Gulf_of_Mexico "Gulf of Mexico"), alleging sabotage by those seeking to promote environmentalism, or a strike by North Korean or Russian submarines. Elements of such theories had been suggested or promoted by US radio host [Rush Limbaugh](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rush_Limbaugh "Rush Limbaugh").[[37]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-37)[[38]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-38)
### New Coke
Main article: [New Coke § Conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Coke#Conspiracy_theories "New Coke")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/f/fa/New_Coke_can.jpg/120px-New_Coke_can.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:New_Coke_can.jpg)[New Coke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Coke "New Coke") was manufactured between 1985 and 2002.
A theory claims that [the Coca-Cola Company](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Coca-Cola_Company "The Coca-Cola Company") intentionally changed to an inferior formula with [New Coke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_Coke "New Coke"), with the intent either of driving up demand for the original product or permitting the reintroduction of the original with a new formula using cheaper ingredients.[[39]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-39) Coca-Cola president [Donald Keough](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Keough "Donald Keough") rebutted this charge: "The truth is, we're not that dumb, and we're not that smart."[[40]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-40)
## Deaths and disappearances
### Death of Nero
In ancient times, widespread conspiracy theories were circulated pertaining to the [death of the Roman emperor Nero](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nero_Redivivus_legend "Nero Redivivus legend"), who killed himself in 68 AD.[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41) Some of these theories claimed that Nero had actually faked his death and was secretly still alive, but in hiding, plotting to reestablish his reign.[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41) In most of these stories, he was said to have fled to the East, where he was still influential.[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41) Other theories held that Nero would return from the dead to retake his throne.[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41) Many [early Christians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Early_Christianity "Early Christianity") feared Nero's return to resume his [vicious anti-Christian persecutions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Persecution_of_Christians_in_the_Roman_Empire#49%E2%80%93250 "Persecution of Christians in the Roman Empire").[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41) The [Book of Revelation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Book_of_Revelation "Book of Revelation") may allude to these conspiracy theories in its description of the slaughtered head returned to life.[[41]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Blount-41)
### John F. Kennedy assassination
Main article: [John F. Kennedy assassination conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_F._Kennedy_assassination_conspiracy_theories "John F. Kennedy assassination conspiracy theories")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/59/JFK_Motorcade_GettyImages-517330536.jpg/250px-JFK_Motorcade_GettyImages-517330536.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:JFK_Motorcade_GettyImages-517330536.jpg)[John F. Kennedy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_F._Kennedy "John F. Kennedy") in the [presidential limousine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Presidential_state_car_\(United_States\) "Presidential state car \(United States\)") shortly before [his assassination](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_John_F._Kennedy "Assassination of John F. Kennedy")
Multiple [conspiracy theories concerning the assassination of John F. Kennedy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_F._Kennedy_assassination_conspiracy_theories "John F. Kennedy assassination conspiracy theories") in 1963 have emerged.[[42]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-42) [Vincent Bugliosi](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vincent_Bugliosi "Vincent Bugliosi") estimated that more than 1,000 books had been written about the Kennedy assassination,[[43]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Bugliosi-43) at least ninety percent of which are works supporting the view that there was a conspiracy.[[43]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Bugliosi-43) As a result of this, the Kennedy assassination has been described as "the mother of all conspiracies".[[44]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Broderick-44)[[45]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Perry-45) The countless individuals and organizations that have been accused of involvement in the Kennedy assassination include the [CIA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Intelligence_Agency "Central Intelligence Agency"), the [Mafia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Italian-American_Mafia "Italian-American Mafia"), sitting [Vice President](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vice_President_of_the_United_States "Vice President of the United States") [Lyndon B. Johnson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lyndon_B._Johnson "Lyndon B. Johnson"), [Cuban Prime Minister](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Cuba "Prime Minister of Cuba") [Fidel Castro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fidel_Castro "Fidel Castro"), the [KGB](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/KGB "KGB"), or even some combination thereof.[[46]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Summers-46)[[43]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Bugliosi-43) It is also frequently asserted that the United States federal government intentionally covered up crucial information in the aftermath of the assassination to prevent the conspiracy from being discovered.[[46]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Summers-46)
### Charlie Kirk assassination
Main article: [Assassination of Charlie Kirk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Charlie_Kirk "Assassination of Charlie Kirk")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/2/2e/Charlie_Kirk_shooting_scene_aerial_view_close_up.png/250px-Charlie_Kirk_shooting_scene_aerial_view_close_up.png)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Charlie_Kirk_shooting_scene_aerial_view_close_up.png)The tent in which Kirk was fatally shot, cordoned off with police tape.
Numerous conspiracy theories about the [assassination](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Charlie_Kirk "Assassination of Charlie Kirk") of [Charlie Kirk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Charlie_Kirk "Charlie Kirk") were posted online.[[47]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Taylor_2025-47) Political consultant [Roger Stone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roger_Stone "Roger Stone") said the attack appeared to have been "a professional hit either by a nation state, rogue elements of our own government or a terrorist organization".[[48]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Thompson_2025-48) One theory, promulgated by Russian state media [RT](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/RT_\(TV_network\) "RT \(TV network\)"), centered on people standing near Kirk, who were purported to have made "unusual gestures" before he was killed.[[47]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Taylor_2025-47)[[48]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Thompson_2025-48) Several senior Russian officials, including former president [Dmitry Medvedev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dmitry_Medvedev "Dmitry Medvedev") and Kremlin special envoy [Kirill Dmitriev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kirill_Dmitriev "Kirill Dmitriev"), speculated on social media about a connection between Kirk's killing and [United States support for Ukraine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/United_States_and_the_Russian_invasion_of_Ukraine "United States and the Russian invasion of Ukraine") because Kirk had been a critic of Western financial and political support for Ukraine in its conflict with Russia.[[49]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-49)[[50]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-50)[[48]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Thompson_2025-48)
Other conspiracy theories include claims that [Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel "Israel") was involved. Many sources, including Israeli media, have stated that these theories are rooted in [antisemitism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitism "Antisemitism").[[51]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-51)[[52]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Berman_2025-52)[[53]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Rosenfeld_2025-53) Some commentators attempted to link the event to the Israeli [Mossad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mossad "Mossad") and to Kirk's comments about the [Epstein files](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Epstein_files "Epstein files"),[[54]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-54)[[52]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Berman_2025-52)[[53]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Rosenfeld_2025-53) and [Maram Susli](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maram_Susli "Maram Susli") resurfaced an August 2025 post by an _[Infowars](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Infowars "Infowars")_ host who stated that Kirk believed "Israel will kill [him] if he turns against them".[[53]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Rosenfeld_2025-53) [Tucker Carlson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tucker_Carlson "Tucker Carlson") was accused by some [pro-Israel groups in the United States](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pro-Israel_groups_in_the_United_States "Pro-Israel groups in the United States") of making antisemitic comments at the service for Kirk by suggesting he supported the conspiracy theory that Jews or Israel were responsible for the assassination. Carlson said that Kirk's assassination reminded him of the [death of Jesus Christ](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Jesus_Christ "Death of Jesus Christ"), whom he said was killed by powerful people for telling the truth.[[55]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-55)[[56]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-56) Israeli prime minister [Benjamin Netanyahu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Benjamin_Netanyahu "Benjamin Netanyahu") rejected the theories, calling them "insane" and issuing multiple video messages claiming no Israeli government involvement in the assassination.[[52]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Berman_2025-52)[[57]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-57)[[58]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-58)
### Disappearance of Harold Holt
Main article: [Disappearance of Harold Holt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disappearance_of_Harold_Holt "Disappearance of Harold Holt")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/40/Harold_Holt_SEATO.jpg/250px-Harold_Holt_SEATO.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Harold_Holt_SEATO.jpg)[Harold Holt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harold_Holt "Harold Holt") in 1966
At approximately 12:20 pm on 17 December 1967, then-[Prime Minister of Australia](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Prime_Minister_of_Australia "Prime Minister of Australia") [Harold Holt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harold_Holt "Harold Holt") disappeared while swimming at [Cheviot Beach](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Cheviot_Beach "Cheviot Beach"), near the town of [Portsea](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Portsea,_Victoria "Portsea, Victoria") in [Victoria](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Victoria_\(state\) "Victoria \(state\)"). While it is presumed that Holt drowned, the topic is still described as one of the biggest unsolved mysteries in Australian history and has spawned many conspiracy theories.[[59]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-59) The conspiracy theories have been widely criticised by the public and by members of Holt's own family. 
In 2007, television presenter [Ray Martin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ray_Martin_\(television_presenter\) "Ray Martin \(television presenter\)") hosted the television special _[Who Killed Harold Holt?](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Who_Killed_Harold_Holt%3F "Who Killed Harold Holt?")_ , which first aired on the [Nine Network](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nine_Network "Nine Network") on 20 November 2007. While the special explored numerous theories about Holt's disappearance, it gave particular credence to the theory that he [killed himself](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide "Suicide").[[60]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-60) In the lead-up to the airing of the program, Holt's biographer, [Tom Frame](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tom_Frame_\(bishop\) "Tom Frame \(bishop\)"), described the allegations made in the special as "unjustified and contrary to all the evidence" in an opinion piece published in _[The Australian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Australian "The Australian")_ newspaper, while Holt's son, Sam, said he was "amazed that people can still keep bringing up [these] fallacious theories". 
Another conspiracy theory is that Holt was actually a spy for the [Chinese government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_government "Chinese government") (under both the [Nationalist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kuomintang "Kuomintang") and [Communist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chinese_Communist_Party "Chinese Communist Party") governments) ever since he began studying at the [University of Melbourne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/University_of_Melbourne "University of Melbourne") in 1929 and that he "pretended to drown", instead theorising that once below the surface two Chinese [frogmen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Frogman "Frogman") transported him to China in a [submarine](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Submarine "Submarine"). This theory also claims that the [Australian Security Intelligence Organisation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Australian_Security_Intelligence_Organisation "Australian Security Intelligence Organisation") (ASIO) covered up any evidence. This theory was first promoted in British writer [Anthony Grey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anthony_Grey "Anthony Grey")'s 1983 novel _[The Prime Minister Was a Spy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Prime_Minister_Was_a_Spy "The Prime Minister Was a Spy")_ , which attracted controversy in Australia. Other conspiracy theories claim that Holt was assassinated. Targets of these claims include [North Vietnam](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/North_Vietnam "North Vietnam") and the US [Central Intelligence Agency](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Intelligence_Agency "Central Intelligence Agency") (CIA).[[61]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-61)
### Death of other prominent figures
The deaths of prominent figures of all types attract conspiracy theorists, sometimes elaborating on historically verified conspiracies such as the assassination of US president [Abraham Lincoln](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Abraham_Lincoln "Assassination of Abraham Lincoln"),[[62]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-62) as well as the deaths of [Martin Luther King Jr.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Martin_Luther_King_Jr.#Conspiracy_theories "Assassination of Martin Luther King Jr."),[[63]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-63) [Mustafa Kemal Atatürk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mustafa_Kemal_Atat%C3%BCrk "Mustafa Kemal Atatürk"),[[64]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-yenisafak-64) [Eric V of Denmark](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eric_V_of_Denmark "Eric V of Denmark"), [Dmitry Ivanovich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tsarevich_Dmitry_Ivanovich_of_Russia_\(born_1582\) "Tsarevich Dmitry Ivanovich of Russia \(born 1582\)"), [Sheikh Rahman](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Sheikh_Mujibur_Rahman "Assassination of Sheikh Mujibur Rahman"), [Yitzhak Rabin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yitzhak_Rabin_assassination_conspiracy_theories "Yitzhak Rabin assassination conspiracy theories"),[[65]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-65) [Zachary Taylor](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zachary_Taylor#Assassination_theories "Zachary Taylor"),[[66]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-66) [George S. Patton](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_S._Patton "George S. Patton"),[[67]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-67)[[68]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-68) [Diana, Princess of Wales](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theories_about_the_death_of_Diana,_Princess_of_Wales "Conspiracy theories about the death of Diana, Princess of Wales"),[[69]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-69) [Dag Hammarskjöld](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dag_Hammarskj%C3%B6ld#Death "Dag Hammarskjöld"),[[70]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-70) [Kurt Cobain](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Suicide_of_Kurt_Cobain "Suicide of Kurt Cobain"), [Michael Jackson](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Michael_Jackson "Death of Michael Jackson"), [Marilyn Monroe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Marilyn_Monroe "Death of Marilyn Monroe"), [Tupac Shakur](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murder_of_Tupac_Shakur "Murder of Tupac Shakur"),[[71]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-vulture.com-71) [Wolfgang Amadeus Mozart](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Wolfgang_Amadeus_Mozart "Death of Wolfgang Amadeus Mozart"),[[72]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-72) [John Lennon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murder_of_John_Lennon "Murder of John Lennon"), [Jimi Hendrix](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Jimi_Hendrix "Death of Jimi Hendrix"), [the Notorious B.I.G.](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Murder_of_the_Notorious_B.I.G. "Murder of the Notorious B.I.G."),[[71]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-vulture.com-71) [Pope John Paul I](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_John_Paul_I "Pope John Paul I"), [Jill Dando](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jill_Dando "Jill Dando"), [Olof Palme](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Olof_Palme "Assassination of Olof Palme"),[[73]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-73) [Linkin Park](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Linkin_Park "Linkin Park") member [Chester Bennington](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Chester_Bennington "Chester Bennington"),[[74]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-74) [Paul Walker](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Walker "Paul Walker"), biological warfare authority [David Kelly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Kelly_\(weapons_expert\) "David Kelly \(weapons expert\)"),[[75]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-75) Haitian president [Jovenel Moïse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Jovenel_Mo%C3%AFse "Assassination of Jovenel Moïse"),[[76]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-76) Indian [freedom fighter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_Indian_independence_activists "List of Indian independence activists") [Subhas Chandra Bose](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Subhas_Chandra_Bose "Death of Subhas Chandra Bose"),[[77]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-77)[[78]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-78)[[79]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-79) and [Bollywood](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bollywood "Bollywood") star [Sushant Singh Rajput](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Death_of_Sushant_Singh_Rajput "Death of Sushant Singh Rajput").[[80]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-80)
There are also claims that deaths were covered up. Such theories include the "[Paul is dead](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_is_dead "Paul is dead")" claim alleging that [Paul McCartney](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_McCartney "Paul McCartney") died in a car accident in 1966 and was replaced by a look-alike Scottish orphan named William Shears Campbell who also went by Billy Shears, and that [the Beatles](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Beatles "The Beatles") hinted at this in their songs, most noticeably "[Revolution 9](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Revolution_9 "Revolution 9")", "[Strawberry Fields Forever](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Strawberry_Fields_Forever "Strawberry Fields Forever")", "[Glass Onion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Glass_Onion_\(song\) "Glass Onion \(song\)")", and "[I Am the Walrus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/I_Am_the_Walrus "I Am the Walrus")", as well on the covers of _[Abbey Road](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Abbey_Road "Abbey Road")_ , _[Sgt. Pepper's Lonely Hearts Club Band](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sgt._Pepper%27s_Lonely_Hearts_Club_Band "Sgt. Pepper's Lonely Hearts Club Band")_ , and _[Magical Mystery Tour](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Magical_Mystery_Tour "Magical Mystery Tour")_.[[81]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-81)[[82]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-82) Another is the conspiracy theory, widely circulated in Nigeria, which alleges that Nigerian president [Muhammadu Buhari](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Muhammadu_Buhari "Muhammadu Buhari") died in 2017 and was replaced by a look-alike Sudanese impostor.[[83]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-TGUK-83)[[84]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-BBCBuhari-84) Many fans of punk-pop star [Avril Lavigne](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avril_Lavigne "Avril Lavigne") claim that she died at the height of her fame and [was replaced by a look-alike named Melissa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avril_Lavigne_replacement_conspiracy_theory "Avril Lavigne replacement conspiracy theory").[[85]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-85) The [Melania Trump replacement theory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melania_Trump_replacement_theory "Melania Trump replacement theory") proposes the same of the US First Lady.[[86]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-86)[[87]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-87)
Inverted theories concerning deaths are also known, prominently claims that [Elvis Presley's death was faked](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Elvis_sightings "Elvis sightings")[[88]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-The_Week-88) and that [Adolf Hitler survived the Second World War](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theories_about_Adolf_Hitler%27s_death "Conspiracy theories about Adolf Hitler's death") and fled to the Americas, to Antarctica, or to the Moon.[[89]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-89) Theories that Hitler had survived are known to have been deliberately promoted by the government of the [Soviet Union](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_Union "Soviet Union") under [Joseph Stalin](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_Stalin "Joseph Stalin") as part of a [disinformation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disinformation "Disinformation") campaign.[[90]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-90)[[91]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-91)[[92]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-92)
The disappearance, and often presumed death, of an individual may also become a cause for conspiracy theorists. Theories of a cover-up surrounding the 1974 [disappearance of Lord Lucan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_Bingham,_7th_Earl_of_Lucan#Ultimate_fate_and_reported_sightings "John Bingham, 7th Earl of Lucan") following the murder of his family's nanny include, for example, allegations of a suicide plot whereby his body was fed to tigers at [Howletts Zoo](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Howletts_Zoo "Howletts Zoo").[[93]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-93)[[94]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-94)[[95]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-95) Numerous conspiracy theories have also attended the 2007 disappearance of English girl [Madeleine McCann](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Disappearance_of_Madeleine_McCann "Disappearance of Madeleine McCann").[[96]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-96)
The murder of [Democratic National Committee](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Democratic_National_Committee "Democratic National Committee") employee [Seth Rich](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Seth_Rich "Seth Rich") spawned several [right-wing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Right-wing "Right-wing") conspiracy theories, including that Rich had been involved with the [leaked DNC emails in 2016](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2016_Democratic_National_Committee_email_leak "2016 Democratic National Committee email leak"), which runs contrary to US intelligence's conclusion that the leaked DNC emails were part of [Russian interference in the 2016 United States elections](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_interference_in_the_2016_United_States_elections "Russian interference in the 2016 United States elections").[[97]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-eugenekiely-97)[[98]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-politifact-98)[[99]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-99) [Law enforcement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Law_enforcement "Law enforcement")[[97]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-eugenekiely-97)[[98]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-politifact-98) as well as [fact-checking websites](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fact-checking_websites "Fact-checking websites") like [PolitiFact.com](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/PolitiFact.com "PolitiFact.com"),[[98]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-politifact-98)[[100]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-politifactgillin-100) [Snopes.com](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Snopes.com "Snopes.com"),[[101]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-:4-101) and [FactCheck.org](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/FactCheck.org "FactCheck.org") stated that these theories were false and unfounded.[[97]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-eugenekiely-97) _[The New York Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_New_York_Times "The New York Times")_ ,[[102]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-fakenewsendures-102) _[Los Angeles Times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Los_Angeles_Times "Los Angeles Times")_ ,[[103]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-shalby-103) and _[The Washington Post](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Washington_Post "The Washington Post")_ called the fabrications [fake news](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fake_news "Fake news") and falsehoods.[[104]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-fakenewsstillworks-104)
## Economics and society
### New World Order
Main article: [New World Order conspiracy theory](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/New_World_Order_conspiracy_theory "New World Order conspiracy theory")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/5/58/Adam_Weishaupt01.jpg/250px-Adam_Weishaupt01.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Adam_Weishaupt01.jpg)[Adam Weishaupt](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adam_Weishaupt "Adam Weishaupt"), founder of the [Bavarian Illuminati](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bavarian_Illuminati "Bavarian Illuminati")
The New World Order theory states that a group of international elites control governments, industry, and media organizations, with the goal of establishing global hegemony. They are alleged to be implicated in most of the major wars of the last two centuries, to carry out secretly staged events, and to deliberately manipulate economies. 
The New World Order has been connected to a wide range of actors including the Illuminati (see [§ Illuminati in Europe](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#Illuminati_in_Europe)), Jews ([§ Antisemitism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#Antisemitism)), colluding world governments or corporations, [NGOs](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Non-governmental_organization "Non-governmental organization") such as the [World Economic Forum](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/World_Economic_Forum "World Economic Forum") ([Great Reset § Conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Great_Reset#Conspiracy_theories "Great Reset")), and secretive organizations such as [Bohemian Grove](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bohemian_Grove "Bohemian Grove"),[[105]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-105) [Le Cercle](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Le_Cercle "Le Cercle"),[[106]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-106) and [Skull and Bones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Skull_and_Bones#Conspiracy_theories "Skull and Bones"). Theorists believe that a wide range of musicians, including [Beyoncé](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Beyonc%C3%A9 "Beyoncé") and [Whitney Houston](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Whitney_Houston "Whitney Houston"), have been associated with the "group".[[107]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-107) Prominent theorists include [Mark Dice](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mark_Dice "Mark Dice") and [David Icke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Icke "David Icke").[[108]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-108)
### Predictive programming
Not to be confused with [Predicative programming](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Predicative_programming "Predicative programming").
See also: [The Simpsons future predictions](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Simpsons_future_predictions "The Simpsons future predictions")
Many theorists allege that the contents of fictional media, in a process called "predictive programming", are manipulated to reference planned [false flags](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/False_flag "False flag"), technological innovations, social changes, and other future events.[[109]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-neuwirthpred-109) These references are understood to be a conditioning and [brainwashing](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Brainwashing "Brainwashing") tool, such that the public becomes more accepting of these events than they would be otherwise.[[109]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-neuwirthpred-109)[[110]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-andrewpseud-110) Predictive programming theories have been used to explain events such as the [September 11 attacks](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/September_11_attacks "September 11 attacks"), [COVID-19 pandemic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/COVID-19_pandemic "COVID-19 pandemic"), and [the assassination of Charlie Kirk](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Assassination_of_Charlie_Kirk "Assassination of Charlie Kirk") and connected with media such as _[Die Hard](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Die_Hard "Die Hard")_ , _[The Simpsons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Simpsons "The Simpsons")_ , _[Contagion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Contagion_\(2011_film\) "Contagion \(2011 film\)"),_ and anime such as _[Blue Gender](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blue_Gender "Blue Gender")_.[[109]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-neuwirthpred-109)[[111]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-111)
### George Soros
Further information: [False or misleading statements by Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/False_or_misleading_statements_by_Donald_Trump "False or misleading statements by Donald Trump") and [List of conspiracy theories promoted by Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories_promoted_by_Donald_Trump "List of conspiracy theories promoted by Donald Trump")
[Hungarian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hungary "Hungary")-American investor [George Soros](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Soros "George Soros") has been the [subject of conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/George_Soros_conspiracy_theories "George Soros conspiracy theories") since the 1990s. Soros has used his wealth to promote many political, social, educational and scientific causes, disbursing grants totaling an estimated $11 billion up to 2016. However, theories tend to assert that Soros is in control of a large portion of the world's wealth and governments, and that he secretly funds a large range of persons and organizations for nefarious purposes, such as [antifa](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antifa_\(United_States\) "Antifa \(United States\)"), which some conspiracy theorists claim is a single far-left militant group. Such ideas have been promoted by [Viktor Orbán](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Viktor_Orb%C3%A1n "Viktor Orbán"), [Donald Trump](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Donald_Trump "Donald Trump"),[[112]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-112) [Rudy Giuliani](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rudy_Giuliani "Rudy Giuliani"),[[113]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-113) [Joseph diGenova](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Joseph_diGenova "Joseph diGenova"),[[114]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-114) [Bill O'Reilly](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bill_O%27Reilly_\(political_commentator\) "Bill O'Reilly \(political commentator\)"), [Roy Moore](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Roy_Moore "Roy Moore"), [Alex Jones](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alex_Jones "Alex Jones"), [Paul Gosar](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Paul_Gosar "Paul Gosar"), and [Ben Garrison](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ben_Garrison "Ben Garrison"). Soros conspiracy theories are sometimes linked to [antisemitic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitism "Antisemitism") conspiracy theories.[[115]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-115)
### Freemasonry
[Conspiracy theories concerning the Freemasons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masonic_conspiracy_theories "Masonic conspiracy theories") have proliferated since the 18th century. Theorists have alleged that Freemasons control large parts of the economies or judiciaries of a number of countries, and have alleged Masonic involvement in the British enquiry regarding the [sinking of the _Titanic_](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Sinking_of_the_Titanic "Sinking of the Titanic") and in the crimes of [Jack the Ripper](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jack_the_Ripper "Jack the Ripper").[[116]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-116)[[117]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-117) Notable among theorists has been American inventor [Samuel Morse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Morse "Samuel Morse"), who in 1835 published [a book of his own conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Morse#Political_views "Samuel Morse").[[118]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-118) Freemason conspiracy theories have also been [linked to certain antisemitic conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Judeo-Masonic_conspiracy_theory "Judeo-Masonic conspiracy theory"). 
### _Üst akıl_
[Conspiracy theories in Turkey](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Conspiracy_theories_in_Turkey "Conspiracy theories in Turkey") started to dominate public discourse during the late reign of the [Justice and Development Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Justice_and_Development_Party_\(Turkey\) "Justice and Development Party \(Turkey\)") and [Recep Tayyip Erdoğan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Recep_Tayyip_Erdo%C4%9Fan "Recep Tayyip Erdoğan").[[119]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-tinfoil-119) In 2014, Erdoğan coined the term _üst akıl_ ("mastermind") to denote the alleged command and control institution, somewhat ambiguously placed with the government of the United States, in a comprehensive conspiracy to weaken or even dismember Turkey, by orchestrating every political actor and action perceived hostile by Turkey.[[120]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-120)[[121]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-121)[[119]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-tinfoil-119) Erdoğan as well as the _[Daily Sabah](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Daily_Sabah "Daily Sabah")_ newspaper have on multiple occasions alleged that very different non-state actors—like the Salafi jihadist [Islamic State of Iraq and the Levant](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamic_State_of_Iraq_and_the_Levant "Islamic State of Iraq and the Levant") (ISIL), the libertarian socialist [Kurdistan Workers' Party](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Kurdistan_Workers%27_Party "Kurdistan Workers' Party") (PKK) and supporters of [Fethullah Gülen](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fethullah_G%C3%BClen "Fethullah Gülen")—were attacking Turkey at the same time in a well-coordinated campaign.[[122]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-globalconspiracy-122)
One instance of promoting the "mastermind" conspiracy theory occurred in February 2017, when then-[Ankara](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ankara "Ankara") Mayor [Melih Gökçek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Melih_G%C3%B6k%C3%A7ek "Melih Gökçek") claimed that earthquakes in the western province of Çanakkale could have been organized by dark external powers aiming to destroy Turkey's economy with an "artificial earthquake" near Istanbul.[[123]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-123) In another example, in November 2017, the [Islamist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Islamism "Islamism") newspaper _[Yeni Akit](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Yeni_Akit "Yeni Akit")_ claimed that the fashion trend of "ripped denim" jeans was in fact a means of communication, via specific forms of rips and holes, between agents of foreign states and their collaborators in Turkey.[[124]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-124)
## Espionage
### Israel animal spying
Main article: [Israel-related animal conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel-related_animal_conspiracy_theories "Israel-related animal conspiracy theories")
There are conspiracy theories alleging that Israel uses animals to conduct espionage or to attack people. These are often associated with conspiracy theories about [Zionism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zionism "Zionism"). Matters of interest to theorists include a series of [shark attacks in Egypt in 2010](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/2010_Sharm_el-Sheikh_shark_attacks "2010 Sharm el-Sheikh shark attacks"), [Hezbollah's](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hezbollah "Hezbollah") accusations of the use of "spying" eagles,[[125]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Okbi-125) and the 2011 capture of a [griffon vulture](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Griffon_vulture "Griffon vulture") carrying an Israeli-labeled [satellite tracking device](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/GPS_wildlife_tracking "GPS wildlife tracking").[[126]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-126)
### Harold Wilson
Numerous persons, including former [MI5](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/MI5 "MI5") officer [Peter Wright](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Peter_Wright_\(MI5_officer\) "Peter Wright \(MI5 officer\)") and Soviet defector [Anatoliy Golitsyn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anatoliy_Golitsyn "Anatoliy Golitsyn"), have alleged that British prime minister [Harold Wilson was secretly a KGB spy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Harold_Wilson_conspiracy_theories "Harold Wilson conspiracy theories"). Historian [Christopher Andrew](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christopher_Andrew_\(historian\) "Christopher Andrew \(historian\)") has lamented that a number of people have been "seduced by Golitsyn's fantasies".[[127]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-127)[[128]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-128)[[129]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-129)
### Malala Yousafzai
Conspiracy theories concerning [Malala Yousafzai](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Malala_Yousafzai "Malala Yousafzai") are widespread in [Pakistan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pakistan "Pakistan"), elements of which originate from a 2013 satirical piece in _[Dawn](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Dawn_\(newspaper\) "Dawn \(newspaper\)")_. These theories variously allege that she is a Western spy, or that her attempted murder by the [Taliban](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Taliban "Taliban") in 2012 was a secret operation to further discredit the Taliban, and was organized by her father and the [CIA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Central_Intelligence_Agency "Central Intelligence Agency") and carried out by actor [Robert de Niro](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Robert_de_Niro "Robert de Niro") disguised as an [Uzbek](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Uzbeks "Uzbeks") [homeopath](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Homeopath "Homeopath").[[130]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-130)[[131]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-131)[[132]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-132)[[133]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-133)
### Antisemitism
Main articles: [Antisemitic trope](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitic_trope "Antisemitic trope") and [International Jewish conspiracy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/International_Jewish_conspiracy "International Jewish conspiracy")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/a/a7/1905_2fnl_Velikoe_v_malom_i_antikhrist.jpg/250px-1905_2fnl_Velikoe_v_malom_i_antikhrist.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:1905_2fnl_Velikoe_v_malom_i_antikhrist.jpg)First edition of _[The Protocols of the Elders of Zion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Protocols_of_the_Elders_of_Zion "The Protocols of the Elders of Zion")_
Since at least the [Middle Ages](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Middle_Ages "Middle Ages"), [antisemitism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antisemitism "Antisemitism") has featured elements of conspiracy theory. In medieval Europe, it was widely believed that [Jews](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jews "Jews") poisoned wells, [had been responsible for the death of Jesus](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_deicide "Jewish deicide"), and [ritually consumed the blood of Christians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blood_libel "Blood libel"). The second half of the 19th century saw the emergence of notions that Jews and/or [Freemasons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Masonic_conspiracy_theories "Masonic conspiracy theories") were plotting to establish control over the world. Forged evidence has been presented to spread the notion that [Jews were responsible for the propagation of communism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Bolshevism "Jewish Bolshevism"), or the hoax _[The Protocols of the Elders of Zion](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Protocols_of_the_Elders_of_Zion "The Protocols of the Elders of Zion")_ (1903), which outlines a supposed high council of Jews planning to control the world.[[134]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-134) Such antisemitic conspiracy theories became central to the worldview of [Adolf Hitler](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Adolf_Hitler "Adolf Hitler"). Antisemitic theories persist today concerning banking,[[135]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Levy_2005_55-135) Hollywood, the news media and a purported [Zionist Occupation Government](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Zionist_Occupation_Government "Zionist Occupation Government") of the United States.[[136]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-136)[[137]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-137)[[138]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-138) These theories all allege plots to establish a world [tyranny](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Tyranny "Tyranny").[[139]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-139)
[Holocaust denial](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Holocaust_denial "Holocaust denial") is also considered an antisemitic conspiracy theory, claiming that the Nazi extermination of European Jews is a [hoax](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Hoax "Hoax") designed to win sympathy for Jews and justify the creation of the [State of Israel](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Israel "Israel").[[140]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-140)[[141]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-141) Holocaust deniers include Iranian president [Mahmoud Ahmedinejad](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mahmoud_Ahmedinejad "Mahmoud Ahmedinejad"),[[142]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-142) the chemist with a conviction for inciting racial hatred [Germar Rudolf](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Germar_Rudolf "Germar Rudolf")[[143]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-143) and the discredited author [David Irving](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Irving_v_Penguin_Books_Ltd "Irving v Penguin Books Ltd").[[144]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-144)
[Reptilian conspiracies](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reptilian_conspiracy_theory "Reptilian conspiracy theory"), a prominent topic in [ufology](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ufology "Ufology") theories, have also been linked to antisemitism as "a very old trope with disturbing links to anti-immigrant and antisemitic hostilities dating to the 19th century."[[88]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-The_Week-88)[[145]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-145)[[146]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-:0-146) Conspiracy author [David Icke](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/David_Icke "David Icke") suggested numerous Jewish political figures as [reptilian shapeshifters](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reptilian_humanoid "Reptilian humanoid") "stating that the Jewish [Rothschild family](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rothschild_family "Rothschild family") is part of a bloodline of reptilian humanoids that secretly control the world".[[147]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-147) Critics contend these theories to be antisemitic although Icke denies animosity towards Jewish people.[[146]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-:0-146) Other far-right [ufologists](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ufology "Ufology") speculate that the [Jewish race](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jewish_Race "Jewish Race") originated from genetic engineering by malevolent extraterrestrials engaged in an interstellar conflict with [Anunnaki](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anunnaki "Anunnaki") or [Pleiadians](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pleiadians "Pleiadians").[[148]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-:1-148)
### Anti-Armenianism
See also: [Anti-Armenianism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Armenianism "Anti-Armenianism") and [Denial of the Armenian Genocide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Denial_of_the_Armenian_Genocide "Denial of the Armenian Genocide")
Conspiracy theories that allege that the Armenians wield secret political power are prevalent in [Azerbaijan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Azerbaijan "Azerbaijan")[[149]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-149) and have been promoted by the government,[[150]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-150) including President [Ilham Aliyev](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ilham_Aliyev "Ilham Aliyev").[[151]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-151)[[152]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-152)[[153]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-153) Turkish foreign minister [Mevlüt Çavuşoğlu](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Mevl%C3%BCt_%C3%87avu%C5%9Fo%C4%9Flu "Mevlüt Çavuşoğlu") has claimed that the Russian media is run by Armenians.[[154]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-154) American writer and disbarred lawyer [Samuel Weems](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Weems "Samuel Weems")[[155]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-155) has claimed that the [Armenian genocide](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Armenian_genocide "Armenian genocide") was a hoax designed to defraud Christian nations of billions of dollars, and that the [Armenian Church](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Armenian_Apostolic_Church "Armenian Apostolic Church") instigates terrorist attacks.[[156]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-156) Filmmaker Davud Imanov has accused the Armenians of plotting against Azerbaijan and has claimed that the [Karabakh movement](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Karabakh_movement "Karabakh movement") was a plot by the [CIA](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/CIA "CIA") to destroy the [Soviet Union](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Soviet_Union "Soviet Union").[[157]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-157)
Main article: [Persecution of Baháʼís](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Persecution_of_Bah%C3%A1%CA%BC%C3%ADs "Persecution of Baháʼís")
[Iran](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Iran "Iran")'s [Baháʼí Faith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Bah%C3%A1%CA%BC%C3%AD_Faith "Baháʼí Faith") minority has been the target of conspiracy theories alleging involvement with hostile powers. Iranian government officials and others have claimed that Baháʼís have been variously agents of the [Russian](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Russia "Government of Russia"), [British](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_the_United_Kingdom "Government of the United Kingdom"), [American](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Federal_government_of_the_United_States "Federal government of the United States") or [Israeli governments](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Government_of_Israel "Government of Israel").[[158]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-un294-158) An apocryphal and historically inaccurate book published in Iran, entitled _The Memoirs of Count Dolgoruki_ , details a theory that the Bahá'ís intend to destroy Islam. Such anti-Baháʼí accusations have been dismissed as having no factual foundation.[[159]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-cooper-159)[[160]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-lifting-160)[[161]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-tavakoli200-161)
### Anti-Catholicism
See also: [Anti-Catholicism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Catholicism "Anti-Catholicism"), [Vatican conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Vatican_conspiracy_theories "Vatican conspiracy theories"), [Jesuit conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jesuit_conspiracy_theories "Jesuit conspiracy theories"), and [Controversies about Opus Dei](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Controversies_about_Opus_Dei "Controversies about Opus Dei")
[![](https://upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/1/19/Anti-Catholic_octopus_cartoon.jpg/250px-Anti-Catholic_octopus_cartoon.jpg)](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/File:Anti-Catholic_octopus_cartoon.jpg)[Anti-Catholic](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Anti-Catholicism "Anti-Catholicism") cartoon depicting [Catholicism](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholicism "Catholicism") as an octopus, from H. E. Fowler and [Jeremiah J. Crowley](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jeremiah_J._Crowley "Jeremiah J. Crowley")'s _The Pope_ (1913)
Since the Protestant [Reformation](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Reformation "Reformation") of the 16th century, theories about Catholic conspiracies have taken many forms, including the 17th-century [Popish Plot](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Popish_Plot "Popish Plot") allegations,[[162]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-162) claims by persons such as [William Blackstone](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/William_Blackstone "William Blackstone") that [Catholics](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_Church "Catholic Church") posed a secret threat to Britain, and numerous writings by authors such as [Samuel Morse](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Samuel_Morse#Political_views "Samuel Morse"), [Rebecca Reed](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Rebecca_Reed "Rebecca Reed"), [Avro Manhattan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Avro_Manhattan "Avro Manhattan"), [Jack Chick](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jack_Chick "Jack Chick") and [Alberto Rivera](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alberto_Rivera_\(activist\) "Alberto Rivera \(activist\)"). Theorists often claim that the [Pope](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope "Pope") is the [Antichrist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antichrist "Antichrist"), accuse Catholics of suppressing evidence incompatible with Church teachings, and describe Catholics as being involved with secret evil rituals, crimes, and other plots. 
In 1853, the Scottish minister [Alexander Hislop](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Alexander_Hislop "Alexander Hislop") published his anti-Catholic pamphlet _[The Two Babylons](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Two_Babylons "The Two Babylons")_ ,[[163]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Grabbe-163) in which he claims that the [Catholic Church](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Catholic_Church "Catholic Church") is secretly a continuation of the [pagan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pagan "Pagan") religion of ancient [Babylon](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Babylon "Babylon"), the product of a millennia-old conspiracy founded by the biblical king [Nimrod](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Nimrod "Nimrod") and the Assyrian queen [Semiramis](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Semiramis "Semiramis").[[163]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Grabbe-163) It also claims that modern Catholic holidays, including [Christmas](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Christmas "Christmas") and [Easter](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Easter "Easter"), are actually pagan festivals established by Semiramis and that the customs associated with them are pagan rituals. Modern scholars have unanimously rejected the book's arguments as erroneous and based on a flawed understanding of Babylonian religion,[[163]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Grabbe-163) but variations of them are still accepted among some groups of [evangelical](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Evangelicalism "Evangelicalism") [Protestants](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Protestantism "Protestantism").[[163]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-Grabbe-163) The [Jehovah's Witnesses](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Jehovah%27s_Witnesses "Jehovah's Witnesses") periodical _[The Watchtower](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Watchtower "The Watchtower")_ frequently published excerpts from it until the 1980s.[[164]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-veith-164) The book's thesis has also featured prominently in the conspiracy theories of [racist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Racist "Racist") groups, such as [The Covenant, The Sword, and the Arm of the Lord](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/The_Covenant,_The_Sword,_and_the_Arm_of_the_Lord "The Covenant, The Sword, and the Arm of the Lord").[[165]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-165)
Fears of a [Catholic takeover of the US](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_in_the_White_House "Pope in the White House") have been especially persistent,[[166]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-166)[[167]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-167) prompted by large-scale Catholic immigration in the 19th century[[168]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-168) and [Ku Klux Klan](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Ku_Klux_Klan "Ku Klux Klan") [propaganda](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Propaganda "Propaganda").[[169]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-169)[[170]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-170) Such fears have attached to Catholic political candidates such as [Al Smith](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Al_Smith "Al Smith")[[171]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-171) and [John F. Kennedy](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/John_F._Kennedy "John F. Kennedy").[[172]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-172)[[173]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-173)[[174]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-174) [Pope John Paul I](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_John_Paul_I "Pope John Paul I") died in September 1978, only a month after his election to the papacy. The timing of his death and the Vatican's alleged difficulties with ceremonial and legal death procedures has fostered [several conspiracy theories](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_John_Paul_I_conspiracy_theories "Pope John Paul I conspiracy theories"). The elderly [Pope Benedict XVI](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Pope_Benedict_XVI "Pope Benedict XVI")'s resignation in February 2013, officially due to "lack of strength of mind and body",[[175]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-175) prompted theories in Italian publications such as _[La Repubblica](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/La_Repubblica "La Repubblica")_ and [_Panorama_](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Panorama_\(magazine\) "Panorama \(magazine\)") that he resigned to avoid the exposure of an underground gay Catholic network.[[176]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-176)[[177]](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_conspiracy_theories#cite_note-177)
### Antichrist
Apocalyptic prophecies, particularly Christian claims about the [end times](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Eschatology "Eschatology"), have inspired a range of conspiracy theories. Many of these cite the [Antichrist](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Antichrist "Antichrist"), a leader who will supposedly create an oppressive world empire.
